Project:

```json

Second:

```json

Ruling: equivalent representation; project retained. The project enters Schedule B line 4 (total interest less excludable savings-bond interest, which the project refuses and so is zero); the second transcription totals the interest payers directly. Schedule B line 2 is the same total over the same payers (Schedule B (2025), lines 1–4), so the two agree within scope.

```json
{"args": [{"args": [{"const": true}, {"ref": "ssb.lump_sum"}], "test": "eq"}, {"args": [{"param": "sales.zero"}, {"ref": "ssb.line1"}], "test": "lt"}], "test": "all"}

Second:

```json

```

Ruling: representation; project retained. The election is for a social security benefit payment for an earlier year, so it can only apply to a filer with benefits; the project tests benefits first (the 'all' stops there) so a filer without benefits is never asked the lump-sum question, and for a filer with benefits the box is the answer itself.

Second transcription's note: Line 27c lets a filer choose not to claim the EIC. No input for that choice was supplied, so a helper input was added. When it is checked, line 27a is zero.

Ruling: scope; project retained. The second adds the filer's choice not to claim the EIC as an input; the tool does not ask it (the line 27c label says so) and enters the credit whenever Steps 1 to 5 pass.

Ruling: scope; project retained. As line 27a: the choice not to claim is not asked; the instructed checks agree in substance (see eic.check_27c).

## Raw second transcription

```yaml
```yaml
lines:
  - id: f1040.line1a
    printed: "1a"
    label: "Total amount from Form(s) W-2, box 1"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: person, field: person.box1}
    refuses: [scope.other_income]
    note: "The instructions say to enter the total of W-2 box 1, including a spouse's on a joint return. The text is open on how to collect that total. Chose to add each person's W-2 box 1 total. The text also excludes inmate wages and nonqualified deferred compensation or section 457 amounts reported in box 1. Those cases are left to the scope.other_income screen."
  - id: f1040.line1z
    printed: "1z"
    label: "Add lines 1a through 1h"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line1a}
    refuses: [scope.other_income]
    note: "Lines 1b through 1h cover household wages, unreported tips, Medicaid waiver payments, dependent care benefits, adoption benefits, Form 8919 wages and other earned income. The tool does not compute these, so they are screened out. Only line 1a is carried."
  - id: f1040.line2a
    printed: "2a"
    label: "Tax-exempt interest"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.tax_exempt_interest, div.box12]}
    note: "The instructions say to include exempt-interest dividends from Form 1099-DIV box 12. The supplied tax-exempt interest input covers only Form 1099-INT and Form 1099-OID, so box 12 is added to it."
  - id: f1040.line2b
    printed: "2b"
    label: "Taxable interest"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: interest, field: interest.amount}
    refuses: [sb.unsupported]
  - id: f1040.line3b
    printed: "3b"
    label: "Ordinary dividends"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: sb.ordinary_dividends}
    refuses: [sb.unsupported]
  - id: f1040.line4a
    printed: "4a"
    label: "IRA distributions"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: retirement
      field: retirement.amount_a
      where: {op: test, eq: [retirement.is_ira, {const: true}]}
    refuses: [retirement.special]
  - id: f1040.line4b
    printed: "4b"
    label: "IRA distributions, taxable amount"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: retirement
      field: retirement.taxable
      where: {op: test, eq: [retirement.is_ira, {const: true}]}
    refuses: [retirement.special]
  - id: f1040.h_ira_rollover_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of IRA distributions of which part or all was rolled over (Exception 1)"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: retirement
      field: retirement.one
      where:
        op: test
        all:
          - {op: test, eq: [retirement.is_ira, {const: true}]}
          - {op: test, eq: [retirement.has_rollover, {const: true}]}
  - id: f1040.line4c
    printed: "4c"
    label: "Check if (box 1) Rollover"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [f1040.h_ira_rollover_count, {const: 0}]}
    note: "This encodes box 1 only (Exception 1). Boxes 2 (QCD) and 3 cover situations outside the supported distributions."
  - id: f1040.line5a
    printed: "5a"
    label: "Pensions and annuities"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: retirement
      field: retirement.amount_a
      where: {op: test, eq: [retirement.is_ira, {const: false}]}
    refuses: [retirement.special]
  - id: f1040.line5b
    printed: "5b"
    label: "Pensions and annuities, taxable amount"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: retirement
      field: retirement.taxable
      where: {op: test, eq: [retirement.is_ira, {const: false}]}
    refuses: [retirement.special]
  - id: f1040.h_plan_rollover_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of pension or annuity distributions with a rollover, including a direct rollover"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: retirement
      field: retirement.one
      where:
        op: test
        all:
          - {op: test, eq: [retirement.is_ira, {const: false}]}
          - {op: test, eq: [retirement.has_rollover, {const: true}]}
  - id: f1040.line5c
    printed: "5c"
    label: "Check if (box 1) Rollover"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [f1040.h_plan_rollover_count, {const: 0}]}
    note: "This encodes box 1 only. Box 2 (the PSO election) and box 3 are not computed."
  - id: f1040.line6a
    printed: "6a"
    label: "Social security benefits"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: ssb.line1}
    refuses: [ssb.terror_disability, ssb.pub915]
  - id: f1040.line6b
    printed: "6b"
    label: "Social security benefits, taxable amount"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: ssb.line18}
    refuses: [ssb.pub915, ssb.lump_sum]
  - id: f1040.line6c
    printed: "6c"
    label: "If you elect to use the lump-sum election method, check here"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: copy, from: ssb.lump_sum}
    refuses: [ssb.lump_sum]
  - id: f1040.line6d
    printed: "6d"
    label: "If you are married filing separately and lived apart from your spouse the entire year, check here"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfs"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [ssb.lived_apart, {const: true}]}
  - id: f1040.line7a
    printed: "7a"
    label: "Capital gain or (loss). Attach Schedule D if required"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: sd.to1040}
    refuses: [sd.other_forms, sd.carryover]
    note: "The text leaves open how line 7a is filled when Schedule D is not required (Exception 1: capital gain distributions only). Chose to take sd.to1040, which is defined as the line 7a amount in both cases."
  - id: f1040.line8
    printed: "8"
    label: "Additional income from Schedule 1, line 10"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: s1.line10}
    refuses: [scope.schedule1]
  - id: f1040.line9
    printed: "9"
    label: "Add lines 1z, 2b, 3b, 4b, 5b, 6b, 7a, and 8. This is your total income"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line1z, f1040.line2b, f1040.line3b, f1040.line4b, f1040.line5b, f1040.line6b, f1040.line7a, f1040.line8]}
  - id: f1040.line10
    printed: "10"
    label: "Adjustments to income from Schedule 1, line 26"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: s1.line26}
    refuses: [scope.schedule1]
  - id: f1040.line11a
    printed: "11a"
    label: "Subtract line 10 from line 9. This is your adjusted gross income"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: f1040.line9, take: f1040.line10}
  - id: f1040.line11b
    printed: "11b"
    label: "Amount from line 11a (adjusted gross income)"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line11a}
  - id: f1040.line12e
    printed: "12e"
    label: "Standard deduction or itemized deductions (from Schedule A)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: sa.deduction}
    refuses: [sa.dependent, sa.unsupported]
    note: "The instructions list exceptions: the dependent worksheet, dual-status alien, and the disaster-loss increase. These are left to the sa.dependent and sa.unsupported screens. The case where a spouse itemizes on a separate return is handled inside sa.deduction."
  - id: f1040.line13a
    printed: "13a"
    label: "Qualified business income deduction from Form 8995 or Form 8995-A"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: qbi.deduction}
    refuses: [qbi.unsupported]
  - id: f1040.line13b
    printed: "13b"
    label: "Additional deductions from Schedule 1-A, line 38"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sales.zero}
    refuses: [scope.schedule1a]
    note: "The tool computes only the case where the filer qualifies for no Schedule 1-A deduction. In that case line 13b has no amount. Any filer who does qualify is refused."
  - id: f1040.line14
    printed: "14"
    label: "Add lines 12e, 13a, and 13b"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line12e, f1040.line13a, f1040.line13b]}
  - id: f1040.line15
    printed: "15"
    label: "Subtract line 14 from line 11b. If zero or less, enter -0-. This is your taxable income"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1040.line11b, take: f1040.line14}
  - id: f1040.h_tax_regular
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Tax on line 15 using the Tax Table or Tax Computation Worksheet"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: {def: tax_on_amount, bind: {amount: f1040.line15, status: f1040.status}, line: tax}}
  - id: f1040.h_no_worksheet
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Exception: neither the Qualified Dividends and Capital Gain Tax Worksheet nor the Schedule D Tax Worksheet is used (no qualified dividends and no net capital gain, or line 15 zero or less)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, le: [qdcg.line4, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, le: [f1040.line15, {const: 0}]}
    note: "The exception is worded for Schedule D lines 15 and 16. That is open for a filer who does not file Schedule D. Chose QDCG worksheet line 4: line 3a plus the smaller of Schedule D lines 15 and 16 floored at zero, or line 7a when Schedule D is not filed. The test is whether that sum is zero or less."
  - id: f1040.h_sdtw_required
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Schedule D Tax Worksheet required: Schedule D line 18 or 19 more than zero and lines 15 and 16 gains, or Form 4952 filed with an amount on line 4g"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [sd.line17, {const: true}]}
            - op: test
              any:
                - {op: test, gt: [sd.line18, {const: 0}]}
                - {op: test, gt: [sd.line19, {const: 0}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [sd.filing4952, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, gt: [f4952.line4g, {const: 0}]}
  - id: f1040.line16
    printed: "16"
    label: "Tax (see instructions). Check if any from Form(s): 1 8814 2 4972 3"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_no_worksheet, {const: true}]}
          then: f1040.h_tax_regular
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_no_worksheet, {const: false}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_sdtw_required, {const: true}]}
          then: sdtw.line47
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_no_worksheet, {const: false}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_sdtw_required, {const: false}]}
          then: qdcg.line25
    refuses: [scope.other_line16, sa.excluded_income]
    note: "The provided text does not give the line 16 method-selection passage. Chose to take the Schedule D Tax Worksheet's own conditions for when it is used. Otherwise the QDCG worksheet is used, and the Tax Table or Tax Computation Worksheet applies under the stated exception. Other line 16 taxes and Form 2555 are screened out."
  - id: f1040.line17
    printed: "17"
    label: "Amount from Schedule 2, line 3"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: s2.line3}
    refuses: [s2.amt]
  - id: f1040.line18
    printed: "18"
    label: "Add lines 16 and 17"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line16, f1040.line17]}
  - id: f1040.line19
    printed: "19"
    label: "Child tax credit or credit for other dependents from Schedule 8812"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: s8812.line14}
  - id: f1040.line20
    printed: "20"
    label: "Amount from Schedule 3, line 8"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: s3.line8}
    refuses: [scope.credits]
  - id: f1040.line21
    printed: "21"
    label: "Add lines 19 and 20"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line19, f1040.line20]}
  - id: f1040.line22
    printed: "22"
    label: "Subtract line 21 from line 18. If zero or less, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1040.line18, take: f1040.line21}
  - id: f1040.line23
    printed: "23"
    label: "Other taxes, including self-employment tax, from Schedule 2, line 21"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: s2.line21}
    refuses: [s2.other]
  - id: f1040.line24
    printed: "24"
    label: "Add lines 22 and 23. This is your total tax"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line22, f1040.line23]}
  - id: f1040.line25a
    printed: "25a"
    label: "Federal income tax withheld from Form(s) W-2"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: person, field: person.box2}
  - id: f1040.line25d
    printed: "25d"
    label: "Add lines 25a through 25c"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line25a, f1040.line25b, f1040.line25c]}
  - id: f1040.h_decline_eic
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Do you not want to claim the earned income credit?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: input, source: "Interview question: line 27c, if you do not want to claim the EIC, check here"}
  - id: f1040.line27a
    printed: "27a"
    label: "Earned income credit (EIC)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_decline_eic, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.zero
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_decline_eic, {const: false}]}
          then: eic.credit
    note: "Line 27c lets a filer choose not to claim the EIC. No input for that choice was supplied, so a helper input was added. When it is checked, line 27a is zero."
  - id: f1040.line27b
    printed: "27b"
    label: "Clergy filing Schedule SE (see instructions)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: copy, from: eic.clergy}
  - id: f1040.line27c
    printed: "27c"
    label: "If you do not want to claim the EIC, check here"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_decline_eic, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.check_27c, {const: true}]}
  - id: "f1040.lineDependents (separated spouses)"
    printed: "Dependents (separated spouses)"
    label: "Check if your filing status is MFS or HOH and you lived apart from your spouse for the last 6 months of 2025, or you are legally separated according to your state law under a written separation agreement or a decree of separate maintenance and you did not live in the same household as your spouse at the end of 2025."
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: copy, from: eic.separated_box}
  - id: f1040.line28
    printed: "28"
    label: "Additional child tax credit (ACTC) from Schedule 8812"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: s8812.line27}
    refuses: [s8812.combat_pay, s8812.puerto_rico]
  - id: f1040.line29
    printed: "29"
    label: "American opportunity credit from Form 8863, line 8"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sales.zero}
    refuses: [scope.credits]
    note: "Form 8863 is not supported. The tool computes only the case with no education credit claimed. Anyone claiming one is refused through scope.credits."
  - id: f1040.line30
    printed: "30"
    label: "Refundable adoption credit from Form 8839, line 13"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sales.zero}
    refuses: [scope.credits]
    note: "Form 8839 is not supported. The tool computes only the case with no adoption credit claimed. Anyone claiming one is refused through scope.credits."
  - id: f1040.line31
    printed: "31"
    label: "Amount from Schedule 3, line 15"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: s3.line15}
  - id: f1040.line32
    printed: "32"
    label: "Add lines 27a, 28, 29, 30, and 31. These are your total other payments and refundable credits"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line27a, f1040.line28, f1040.line29, f1040.line30, f1040.line31]}
  - id: f1040.line33
    printed: "33"
    label: "Add lines 25d, 26, and 32. These are your total payments"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line25d, f1040.line26, f1040.line32]}
  - id: f1040.line34
    printed: "34"
    label: "If line 33 is more than line 24, subtract line 24 from line 33. This is the amount you overpaid"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1040.line33, take: f1040.line24}
    note: "When line 33 is not more than line 24, the form leaves line 34 blank. Chose to treat blank as zero."
  - id: f1040.line37
    printed: "37"
    label: "Subtract line 33 from line 24. This is the amount you owe"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1040.line24, take: f1040.line33}
    note: "The printed text is a plain subtraction. When line 33 is more than line 24 there is an overpayment on line 34 and nothing is owed. Chose to floor the result at zero."
```
